# Status Log — Amrutanjan Homepage Sprint

**Created:** 2026-08-07
**Tracks:** 32 PRDs across 6 phases
**Gate:** `DEFINITION-OF-DONE.md`

Living document. Update it as each PRD moves, not in a batch at the end — the value here is the
record of what actually happened, which nobody can reconstruct afterwards.

## How to use this log

**Status vocabulary** — use exactly these, nothing else:

| Status | Meaning |
|--------|---------|
| `Not Started` | No work begun |
| `In Progress` | Picked up, not yet passing its own acceptance criteria |
| `Blocked` | Cannot proceed. **Must** have a matching row in the Blocker Log below |
| `Done` | Every acceptance criterion in the PRD is verified, not merely intended |
| `Done with deviations` | Shipped, but something differs from the PRD. **Must** have a row in the Deviations & Decisions Log |

**Rules**

- `Done` means verified. If a criterion was skipped, the status is `Done with deviations` and the skip is logged. Do not round up.
- A PRD cannot be `Done` while its upstream dependencies are not — check the Depends On column.
- Anything decided during execution that is not already written in a PRD or TDD goes in the Deviations log. Undocumented decisions are how the next person builds the wrong thing.
- When a PRD updates a TDD, tick it in the Documentation Obligations table so the v1.3 bump does not get lost.

### Phase 4 — Chrome

| 26 | 4.1 header-restyle | 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4, 1.1 | Done with deviations | 2026-08-13 | 🔴 **THIS PRD FIXED A LIVE SITE-WIDE DEFECT THAT THE THEME-WIDE RENAME SWEEP INTRODUCED — the header menu was rendering ZERO links on every page of the store.** `sections/header.liquid:27` declares `header_menu` (renamed from `menu` by commit `514ca07`), but both snippets still read `section.settings.menu`, so `linklists[nil].links` was nil and the loop iterated zero times. Proven in rendered HTML before the fix: desktop nav was `<ul class="…gap-6"></ul>` and the mobile drawer `<ul class="divide-y…"></ul>`, both empty. **Cause: the rename script skipped `section.settings.*` inside snippets on the reasoning that `{% render %}` isolates scope — true generally, FALSE when `section:` is passed explicitly**, which `header.liquid:6,9` does. A sweep confirmed `header-desktop` and `header-mobile` are the theme's **only two** snippets receiving `section:`; every other occurrence sits in a `{% doc %}` example, so the blast radius was the header alone. Fixed in both snippets; nav now renders. **The rename-sweep row's "0 unresolved references" claim is corrected below.** Theme check **134/0**, **upload verified clean**. **0 files created, 5 modified** (`header-desktop.liquid`, `header-mobile.liquid`, `sections/header.liquid`, `assets/base.css`, `config/settings_data.json`). ✅ **ZERO NEW SCHEMA SETTINGS — Sprint §1 held**: `git diff sections/header.liquid` is a **single line** (the border removal), nothing inside `{% schema %}`. ✅ **Desktop measured exact**: band **110** white, no bottom border, logo **100×100 bottom-flush** (0px from band bottom), nav **DM Sans 16px/24px weight 500, `tracking: normal`, `rgb(23,23,23)`, opacity 1**, gaps **40/40**, nav centred to **−2px**, icons **24×24 ×3**. 🔴 **DEVIATION 1 — `settings.logo_width` resolves to the SCHEMA default (120), not nil.** The plan specified `{{ settings.logo_width | default: 100 }}`, assuming an unset setting yields nil so the Liquid filter fires. It does not: Shopify resolves `settings_schema.json`'s default first, so the logo rendered **120×120** and pushed the nav **8px** off centre. Fixed by storing `logo_width: 100` in `settings_data.json` (a value, **not** a schema change) and pinning mobile to a fixed 58px, since one setting cannot express two breakpoint sizes. **Desktop stays merchant-controllable; mobile no longer is.** 🔴 **DEVIATION 2 — the plan's "mobile sizes to content" decision was UNSOUND, and devx caught it on a real device.** The band rendered **24px** and the 58px logo **overflowed into the hero by 17px**. The logo is centred with an `absolute left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2` wrapper, so it is **out of flow and contributes no height**; the band was sized by the 24px icon group. `w-[58px]` had been working all along. Fixed with `min-h-[58px]`; verified band **58**, logo **58×58**, **0px overflow**, centred 0 on both axes. 🔴 **MY MOBILE VERIFICATION METHOD MISSED IT.** With the viewport pinned at 1920 and `resize_window` ignored, mobile was measured by force-showing the `lg:hidden` subtree at desktop width — which reports element sizes correctly but not band behaviour in a real mobile layout. **Forced-visibility measurement is not a substitute for rendering at 390px.** ✏️ **Hamburger rebuilt 2 bars → 3** (17×2, 5px gaps) per the design; `base.css` open-state reworked so outer bars rotate ±45°/±7px and the middle fades. One-class revert. ✏️ **`font-heading` → `font-primary`** on both logo text fallbacks — it is a **dead class**, defined nowhere in `tailwind.config.js` or `base.css`. ⚠️ It survives in **3 other files** (`featured-collection.liquid:17`, `footer.liquid:29`, `newsletter.liquid:8`), out of scope here. ⚠️ The fallback path is now unexercised, since a logo was set. ⚠️ **Store write**: the circular logo was found in the Figma extraction as `figmaAssets/mobile-menu-image-2.png` (250×250 RGBA — Figma misnames the desktop header frame "Mobile Menu"), uploaded as **`amrutanjan-header-logo.png`** and set as `settings.logo`. phase by decision — recorded here so they surface before 5.2 and 5.3, not after.
